# Files kept out of main

The reproduction branches committed every artifact below GitHub's 100 MB limit. When their content moved under
`applications/alphafold/` on main, the files below were left out: itemset and data tables, copies of third-party
data, compiled binaries, campaign state markers, the review notes of the paper and the repository-root files of the
branches. Paths are given as they would be on main (the branches' root files as they were).

Where the files are: in the author's unpublished archive, made of the local branches
`alphafold-experimental-results-reproduction` at `8c1594c` (tag `archive/alphafold-experimental-results-reproduction`; the column says `archive, GPU branch`) and
`alphafold-cpu-reproduction` at `fa8be0e` (tag `archive/alphafold-cpu-reproduction`; `archive, CPU branch`), and a git bundle of both. The GPU
branch was also pushed to origin; that copy is not guaranteed to stay.

Re-deriving them: the CPU run's full-data itemset tables (C1-C6, C3G, M4, S1-S3, X1) match the GPU run's
itemset for itemset and count for count (`runs/20261019T1718Z-cpu/CPU_COMPARISON.md`), so either run's scripts
re-derive both. The CPU run's null-model (600 files), G0 (98) and rules (3) tables have no GPU counterpart: G0
covers other proteins and the null permutations match the GPU's only as a distribution (`CPU_REPORT.md`); they
are re-derivable with the CPU run's scripts, the null permutations from the `perm_seed` and `shuffle_seed` in
their `meta.json`. The third-party copies are the Gene Ontology `go.obo` of release
`releases/2026-07-26` (used by the K=22 parent-child check) and the InterPro2GO mapping of version
`2026/07/06 18:13:03` (used by the citation audit), the smoke-test inputs that
`runs/20260902T0000Z/phase2/scripts/smoke_extract.sh` regenerates, and the download probes of
`runs/20260902T0000Z/README.md` (an AlphaFold metadata shard and the UniProt keyword list), copies of public files.

Files above 100 MB that were never committed are listed in each run's `EXCLUDED_LARGE_FILES.md`.
